# Fact sheet

Generated by `scripts/thesis/make_numbers.py`. One row per LaTeX macro in `numbers.tex`.
Seeds in macro names: SeedA = 42, SeedB = 1337, SeedC = 2024. Variants: ZS zero-shot, FT fine-tuned (mean of three seeds unless a Seed suffix is present; SD = sample standard deviation), CL clarify-trained (seed 42 only).

Total macros: 6460. Missing: 0.
